test "built_ins/list/immutable/splice/original" {
    try support.check(.values, .{ .input = .{.items = &.{2, 0, 1}, .index = 0, .count = 0, .replacement = &.{-1}, .value = 3}, .later = .{.items = &.{2, 0, 1}, .index = 0, .count = 1, .replacement = &.{-1}, .value = 3}, .expected = &.{-1, 2, 0, 1}, .following = &.{-1, 0, 1} });
